There is one fallback now, "Unlisted item", in all of them; Solari keeps its name and the id stays a data attribute for the picture lookup only. All 27 House swatches and the 96 pictured reward items are already in the item catalog, and the repository holds no other list of real reward items, so no name was added.
- **Imperial Victory Analysis says the House count once** (fixes #1052; display only: no request or save field changed; checked in a headless browser, not in the game). The panel showed the count three times (the headline "ATREIDES LEAD 12 TO 4 · 9 HOUSES OPEN", the row "Atreides 12 · Harkonnen 4 · Open 9" and the sentence "House count: Atreides ahead 12 to 4."), and the plaque above already shows 12 and 4. The headline stays; the other two lines are gone, and so is the sentence "House count incomplete" (the headline says it). "Lines still open: 4 · Atreides 4 · Harkonnen 0" is now "Lines of five still possible: Atreides 4 · Harkonnen 0", and is not drawn when a House cannot be read. A won panel keeps its how-line.
- **House Intelligence: a faction with no row in the save reads 0 / 4,000, not "not known / 4,000"** (fixes #1048; display only; checked in a headless browser, not in the game). FACTION HOUSE CONTROL shows 0 for a faction the save has no contribution row for in that House, like a House with no contribution of yours. The distinction is kept in the hover text only ("No record of this faction in the save for this House."). A goal the save does not hold still reads "not known". The Classic list and the House hover card already showed 0 there.
- **House Intelligence ledger: the header says TIER and REWARD on one line and lines up with the rows** (fixes #1047; display only; checked in a headless browser, not in the game). "TIER & THRESHOLD" wrapped over two lines; it is "TIER" now, since each row shows its threshold under it. The header labels are 12 px, never wrap, and the header has the border box of a row, so REWARD starts where the reward text of the rows below starts (it sat a few pixels to the left).
- **House Intelligence ledger: two tiles, "Tiers earned x / 5" and "Collected x / y"** (fixes #1046; the "0 OF 5 EARNED" tag of #1059 goes with it; display only: no request or save field changed; checked in a headless browser, not in the game). The four tiles (Tiers earned, Collected, Not collected, Not reached) are two. Collected reads x / y with y the number of earned tiers, so a House with nothing earned says 0 / 0. "Not reached" was only the total minus the earned count, and "Not collected" is y minus x: both stay in the tooltip and the screen-reader text ("3 of 5 tiers earned · 1 of 3 earned tiers collected, 1 waiting at the house representative"). The tag "N OF 5 EARNED" beside the ledger heading, which wrapped to two lines and repeated the tile, is gone; a House with no tier record keeps a short tag, "FAVOR WON · NO TIER RECORD". The line under the board says "N of 25 tiers earned · C of N collected (W waiting at the house representatives)". The Classic list draws no such tiles and is unchanged.
- **House Intelligence: Top contributors are the names only, numbered, with no amounts and no "Unnamed contributor" rows** (fixes #1058; same intent as #1043 for the banner; display only: no request or save field changed; checked in a headless browser, not in the game). The line "Contributors to this House ... Unnamed contributor 1,000" under the two House control bars is now a short numbered list under TOP CONTRIBUTORS (up to five names, the biggest first, no amounts). A contributor the save gives no name for is left out; when none has a name nothing is drawn, and a House with no contributor row at all still says "Top contributors: not in the save". The tooltip and screen-reader text of the section name the same names without amounts. Help (The Landsraad board) says so.
- **The count "25 of 25 Houses shown" is gone from the board when no filter is on** (fixes #1044; display only: no behaviour, request or save field changed; checked in a headless browser, not in the game). The status node stays on the page, empty, as the polite live region.
- **The board header under "The Landsraad" says just "Term 7"** (fixes #1033; wording only: no behaviour, request or save field changed). "Fixed 5 × 5 board · game positions read from board_index" is gone from the header; Help (The Landsraad board) says the numbers are the game's own positions, read from board_index. A board that is not a fixed 5 by 5 still says so.
- **The row "Faction race" of the House hover card is now "Faction standing"** (fixes #1023; wording only: no behaviour, request or save field changed). The card of a House name on the board and in the Classic list, and the tooltip of the House name button, say "faction standing".
- **Landsraad board: a House won by a faction no longer says "Favor won - no contribution of yours"; it shows the count of any other House** (fixes #1022; display only: no request, route or save field changed, and a House whose favor is won stays unavailable to write exactly as before; nothing checked in the game). The card, the list row and the House panel of a House that a faction won and where the character has no row show 0/5 EARNED with the check and diamond counts (the real numbers when the character has contributions), the list says "Not started" and "0 of 5 tiers earned", and the panel shows its four summary tiles. The faction line, the tier boxes and the card state are unchanged, and a screen reader still hears "favor won by" the faction. A House of a faction with no tier rows in the save still says "Favor won - no tier record in the save".
- **Specialization XP: Add XP accepts only whole numbers from 1 up; removing XP is only the Reset buttons** (fixes #1032; SAVE WRITE PATH validation change, so reviewed before it is merged; decided by the operator on 2026-11-19; not checked in the game). The label is "Add XP" (was "Add or remove XP (a negative number removes)"). The box has min 1, does not take the minus key, and says under itself when what is typed is zero, negative, not a whole number or over the largest XP (the same sentence as the toast: "Type a whole number of XP from 1 to 44,182. XP is added here, never removed (use Reset to take it back). Nothing was sent."); the classic list now stops the same numbers before it sends (it used to leave that to the write path). The write path (`AddSpecXP` in `internal/ops/specialization.go`) now takes an amount from 1 to 44,182 only: zero, any negative number and anything that is not a whole number are refused with a plain message and nothing is written (before, -44,182 to -1 took XP off, clamped at 0). Positives behave as before, including 1e3 and 1.0, and the cap still clamps the total. The tip and the Help page carry the new range. The recorded-request golden of the Specialization page changes in one scenario only: "Remove XP -1000" (it sent amount -1000) is now "Remove XP -1000: refused, nothing is sent" in both views.
- **Command Center Specializations: the XP amount rule and the reset rule are a hover or focus tip, no longer always-visible text** (fixes #1030; display only, decided by the operator on 2026-11-19: no validation, limit or request changed, the request goldens are unchanged). The sentence under the XP box and the sentence under Reset XP of All Known Tracks leave the page. Each is now the tip of the control and of a small **i** mark beside it (the existing tip pattern: shown on hover and on keyboard focus, Escape closes it, the controls and the mark point to it with aria-describedby, and the mark is a 44 px focusable button so a touch screen can open it). The full wording is in the Help tab, Specializations page. A reset that has nothing to reset keeps its one visible sentence.
- **Command Center Specializations: the stat labels XP ACQUIRED and KEYSTONES PURCHASED read XP and KEYSTONES** (fixes #1031; wording only, decided by the operator on 2026-11-19: the values keep their "current / total" form, nothing is written and no request or field changed). The same word change is made on the overview tile. The bars keep their names for a screen reader ("Experience of the selected path", "Keystones purchased on the selected path").
- **Command Center board: the winner's circle in the Imperial Victory Analysis panel has room under the SYSSELRAAD WON box** (refs #1021; display only: the words are unchanged). The box's gold win outline (3 px, 3 px off the box) and its glow reached into the 8 px above the circle, so the circle came within about 2 px of the outline. The circle now sits 15 px below the box (13 px on a phone), about 9 px clear of the outline: the same clear space as between the heading and the box and between the circle and the title.
- **Command Center plaque: the 13th seat is redone from measurements of the approved artwork** (refs #983 and #982; display only: nothing is written and no request, field or Go code changed; requested by the operator on 2026-11-19 after the first build was judged nothing like the approved design; to be tested; not checked in the game). The first build was drawn from the wording of the issues, and drew a tall pointed vesica: the ring was 80 units across with two wedges as tall as the ring was wide. The approved artwork (2172 by 724, the same coordinate system as the drawing) was measured instead: the ring is 112 units across (2.8 times an ordinary seat of 40) with a 5.5 unit rim and touches the rail on both sides; the upward wedge on its top is the small one (30 wide, 18 above the ring, tip just above the rail) and the downward wedge is the bigger one (43 wide, 28 below the ring, hanging past the rail), about 1.7 to 1 and 1.5 to 1 wide to tall (not the 3 to 1 of the issue, and not mirror images); the ring is a whole circle that runs over the wedge bases and the wedge sides start on it (still one path); the interior is dark brown with an orange light inside its rim; the gem is 70 by 94 units (0.63 of the ring wide, not 0.4), a pale bevel round four facets with a pale cross, 2 units above the upward wedge's tip. The seats stay 40 units across and the gaps between outer edges stay equal (#965), so the neighbours move 4 units further out; the crest inside a claimed seat is 64 units. The rails are unchanged. Pictures of the build beside the approved artwork are in the pull request.
- **The plain Special Vendor decree is shown nowhere on the Landsraad page, and on a phone Term and Decrees comes after the board as a closed disclosure** (operator order; fixes #933; display only: nothing is written and no request, field, route or Go code changed; not checked in the game or on a phone). The plain `SpecialVendorActive` row does nothing in the game. The closed list that #634 put under the decree pool ("Hidden: the plain SpecialVendorActive row ...") is gone, and the row is no longer in the Active decree drop-down, in the pool count or in any wording, in the Command Center and in the Classic list. It stays in the save and in the data as it is and the editor never changes or deletes it; the four category vendors (Vehicles, Weapons, Armor, Utilities) stay. If a term holds the plain row as its active or next decree, the page says "Another decree" with no id and no effect line (the plaque too), the drop-down keeps that one value, and Apply writes the same id back, never none. The recorded-request test lost its two scenarios on the hidden row (Make active and Set flag), since the row has no control any more; no other recorded request or golden changed. On a phone (a window 640 px wide or less) the Term and Decrees section is drawn after the board and the panels at its side, so the Houses come first, and everything under its heading is one disclosure that is closed until Show (its line says the term, the reigning faction and the active decree); a wide window shows it open with no summary, as before; the choice made on a phone lasts over a redraw. It is a real `details` element: Tab, Enter and Space work, the summary is 44 px high and has the focus ring.
- **Command Center plaque: the 13th seat is a ring with a wedge on top and one below and a separate faceted gem above, as in the approved design** (fixes #983 and #982; display only: nothing is written and no request, field or Go code changed; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18, to be tested; not checked in the game). The middle seat was a small filled gold disc inside a thin halo, with a large plain diamond far above the track and a small triangle under it. It is now ONE outline: a bright gold ring twice as wide as an ordinary seat (outer radius 40 against 20) with a dark interior and a warm glow inside and outside, an UPWARD wedge on the top of the ring and a DOWNWARD wedge on its bottom, mirror images of each other, drawn as a single closed path so the rim runs from the ring into the wedge sides without a seam. The wedges are filled with a vertical gold gradient (deeper at the base corners, pale at the tip), reach the plaque's rails (the rails and their colour, thickness and glow are unchanged and run behind) and hang a few units past them, with clear space under the lower tip (#982). Above the upward wedge floats a separate faceted diamond gem of four facets, about 40 percent of the ring's width, a 2 unit gap above the wedge tip. A claimed middle seat shows the white crest on the dark interior, and a win by 13 Houses keeps the bold ring and the travelling arc (the arc now stops at the wedges). The equal-gap rule of #965 is kept: the gap from the ring's outer edge to its two neighbours is the same as every other gap (the wedges run up and down, so they take no room from it), and the symmetry of #971 is kept. The geometry helpers (`TRACK.halo`, `TRACK.wedge`, `TRACK.wedgeHalf`, `seatX`, `seatOuter`) and the seat tests are updated, not relaxed: the gap and edge tests read the outline's arcs, and new tests check the ring is 1.8 to 2 times a seat, the outline is one closed symmetric path with mirrored wedges whose base corners lie on the ring, the tips pass the rails, and the gem gap is 1 to 2 units. Forced colours draw the same shapes without the glow.
- **Command Center board: House squares have the House hover card, rewards show their picture, Jump to House lists names only, and the WON tag takes the gold win outline** (fixes #975, #977, #979, #978; display and wording only: nothing is written and no request, field or Go code changed, the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18, to be tested; not checked in the game). (1) Hovering a House square of the 5x5 board, or putting the keyboard focus on it, opens the same House hover card as the list rows (name, next reward, not collected, faction race, the place as a link to the Saved map), built by the same page code (`LCC_HOST.card`), closed by Escape; the tier boxes inside the square keep their own mouse-over, and a touch screen, which has no hover, keeps the Inspector. (2) The reward mouse-over of a tier box, an Inspector tier row and the list's Tiers, Next tier and Not collected cells shows the item picture, its display name, the quantity and the tier with its state; the game's internal item name is shown and spoken nowhere any more (the Classic tier text lost its "Game id" too). 96 reward item pictures (64 px WebP, scaled from the Dune Docker console item pictures read from the local checkout, served from this program's own address, no network request) are listed in the art manifest and the art notice; a reward with no picture (the 25 House swatches have none) shows the name only, and Solari keeps no mouse-over. (3) The Jump to House drop-down lists the House names only, A to Z, without the square number. (4) The SYSSELRAAD WON tag of the victory panel uses the one gold double outline with glow of the winning Houses (a solid line, with the same slow pulse under Motion Full and none under reduced motion; forced colours keep a solid double border); a dashed outline stays for what is not settled.
- **Term and Decrees: the term's time is one row, ENDS IN 18 h 37 m, and the decree elected during the term is called Next term's decree** (#981; display only: the same save reads, no request changed). The Starts row, the play-clock seconds, the length of the term, the real time of the save and the line about calendar dates are gone from the section, the Earlier terms and the classic list. The value is the term's end on the play clock minus where the play clock stood when the save was written, in hours and minutes (under an hour "37 m", under a minute "less than a minute", at or past the end "ended", unreadable "not known"); its tooltip says it counts down only while the game is running, as of the last save. The elected decree reads "Next term's decree", "none yet" before the vote, in the section, the pool tags and the classic list.
- **Command Center banners: TOP CONTRIBUTORS shows every name whole, with its amount, and the character by name** (fixes #972; display and wording only: nothing is written and no request, field or Go code changed; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18, to be tested; not checked in the game). The operator's complaint was the layout: a banner line was one piece of text, "name · amount", cut with an ellipsis ("Fortune Hunt..."), so the end of the name and the amount were never visible. The data was checked against a read-only copy of a save and was right (the ranking is the sum of each contributor's rows over all the Houses of the term, the biggest first, equal amounts by name; in that save the Atreides banner is a four-way tie of 1,000 among guilds, so its order is the alphabet), and the wording was only incomplete. A line is now a name and an amount of its own, run on as text: a name wraps onto a second line (an unbroken one too), the amount stays whole behind its last word, there is no ellipsis anywhere in the list, and the full name is also the mouse-over text of the name. In the wide plaque the type is 11 px or more and the list is a column that wraps, so an entry that does not fit in the height of the cloth is out of sight whole instead of cut through (at 1280 px and wider all three show, even with the longest guild names of the save; at 1024 px two or one, depending on the names). The mouse-over text of the heading says that the amounts are added up over all the Houses of the term, that equal amounts are listed by name, A to Z, that progress the save records without a contributor is not named, and gives the three whole ("Top 3: 1. Bravo Squad 1,500; ..."), so a cloth that shows fewer loses nothing. The character's own row now shows the character's name from the save (the same field the board uses elsewhere), on the banners, in the House panel and in the inspector; "You" only when the name cannot be read. Forced colours use the text colour for the amount. Tests: `web-tests/lcc-contributors.test.mjs` (the ranking against an independent sum of the raw rows, the longest guild names of the save, the character name and its fallback, the line structure, the style rules, the heading text), and `scripts/lcc-m7-sweep.mjs` now fails on a banner line that is cut through.
- **Command Center plaque: every one of the 25 seats is a symmetric ring** (fixes #971, after #964 and #965 evened the gaps; display only: nothing is written and no request, field or Go code changed; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18, to be tested; not checked in the game). The right side of the open seats and of the won seats looked darker, as if the circle were cut off. It was not a clip (every seat is drawn inside its own box, nothing is cut by the drawing, the track or the plaque, at device scales 1, 1.25, 1.5 and 2) but the lighting: the open, claimed and gold seats were filled with a gradient lit from the upper left, and the two round medallion pictures of the claimed seats carry lighting of their own (a bright rim on one side). Measured in a real browser engine as the mean light of the left and the right half of the rim, the right half was 19 percent darker on a won Atreides seat, 17 percent on an open seat and 15 percent on a won Harkonnen seat. The four seat gradients are now centred on the seat (the edge is one colour all the way round), and a flat ring of the faction colour is drawn over the rim of a medallion; the right half is now within 1.5 percent of the left (the rest is the track's own colour behind it). Forced colours hide the ring together with the pictures. Tests: `web-tests/lcc-seat-symmetry.test.mjs`.
- **Command Center Specializations: a path at level 100 has the same gold double outline as a winning House, and the lanes and the dossier show the clean path emblems with no box** (fixes #988 and #989; display only: nothing is written and no request changes, the request golden files are unchanged; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18; checked in a headless render at 1280 and 390 px in both themes and with reduced motion, not in the game). A path lane at the XP cap or the full path now takes the one win outline of the Landsraad board (the same rule, outline token, glow dial and slow pulse; the pulse runs only with Motion Full and no reduced-motion request, and the outline stays still otherwise); the old static inner line and glow are gone, and a path below 100 keeps a normal border. The chosen path is marked by a light bar along the foot of its card, so it stays apart from the gold outline. The lane picture is the clean emblem of the path (about 72 px) and the dossier picture the same emblem at 96 px, in place of the scroll banner picture in a bordered square; the scroll pictures (`art/lanes`) are still used by the Landsraad board, so they stay.
- **Command Center: "earned" and "collected" run through the whole board** (finishes #962; display text only: nothing is written, no request or field changes; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18; not checked in the game). The list cell reads "3 of 5 tiers earned" and the next-tier cell "top tier earned"; House Intelligence says "3 OF 5 EARNED" in the ledger header, "Tiers earned" in its four counts, "top personal tier earned" under the contribution, and "earned" in its summary, the "Important" note, the "delivery unknown" explanation and the mouse-over and screen-reader text of the tier boxes, the cards and the Term section meter. The words are written once, in the wording table `WORDS` of `lcc-model.js`. The tier state word NOT REACHED is unchanged (whether it becomes NOT EARNED is still an open question, because it also changes the Classic list), and the Classic list keeps its own words.
- **Command Center list: "favor won", Square and Specialization, one mark in the favor cell, the list opens by House name, and the list has the Classic mouse-overs** (fixes #952, #950, #953, #951, #940 and #937; display and wording only: nothing is written and no request, field or Go code changed, the recorded request golden files are unchanged; checked in a headless render and in the page tests, not in the game). A faction does not own or claim a House, it wins its favor: the list column "Owner" is "Favor won by" (the cell says the faction, or "Not won"), the panel band says "FAVOR WON · HARKONNEN" or "FAVOR NOT WON", the filter chips are Any, Not won and Favor won, and the legend, the card line, the accessible names and the notes follow. "No." is "Square" and "Path" is "Specialization" (also "By specialization", the tooltip of the House name and, in the Classic list, the number of a row). The words are written once, in a wording table beside the Sysselraad words in `lcc-model.js`. The favor cell shows the faction name alone: the letter that stood before it read as a second H over HARKONNEN when the cell wrapped. The list opens sorted by House name, A to Z. The House name of a row carries the Classic hover card (built by the same function as the Classic list's), the Location cell the Classic place link with the quick map peek, and the progress, tiers, next-tier and not-collected cells the Classic words; the Place line of the House card is a link that opens the Saved map (Classic and Command Center), and the card stays open while the pointer or the keyboard focus is on it. Each reward row of the panel shows its item on hover and on focus (name, amount, game id, state), and Solari shows none. The Classic list and the sentences the two views share (a result line, the tooltip of Complete) still say "taken" and "Open"; the Specializations tab still calls a lane a path.
- **Command Center: one wording for who won the Sysselraad** (fixes #929, #926 and #931; display only: nothing is written, no request, field or Go code changed; checked in a headless render at six widths in both themes, not in the game). A faction that holds a line of five or 13 or more Houses is now shown as the winner: "SYSSELRAAD WON · <FACTION>" with "BY FIVE IN A LINE" or "BY 13 HOUSES"; with no winner the board says "SYSSELRAAD CONTESTED" and the count ("HARKONNEN LEAD 11 TO 4 · 10 HOUSES OPEN"); when both factions qualify it says "SYSSELRAAD UNDECIDABLE" with the reason. The save keeps no winner and no vote, so the facts line says the game works the winner out from the Houses. One function and one table of words serve the plaque, the Imperial Victory Analysis panel, the card tag (on the Houses of a held line), the legend (one key, WINNING HOUSES · SYSSELRAAD WON, in place of VICTORY LINE and MAJORITY HOUSE · COUNT NOT VERIFIED), the Strategy view line about previews, the list badge, the Strategy view, the screen-reader text and the tooltips. The old headline "VOTING SESSION · MAJORITY AT 13 OF 25 HOUSES" is gone, and so is every use of the words Imperial Dominion. In the victory panel the title slot carries the headline, the winner's own crest shows in its colour (no icon when nobody has won), the explanatory paragraph is removed and the last line is the how-line, and the folded list of twelve lines is removed (the lines stay in the Sysselraad Strategy view). The Houses that make up a win, the five of a line or the 13 or more Houses of the winner, now share one gold outline (the double outline the majority Houses had, with a stronger glow and, with Motion Full and no reduced-motion request, a slow pulse of the glow) and one flag, SYSSELRAAD WON; the separate line outline and majority outline are gone. The gold centre seat of the seat track (the 13th) also gets the win ring, and the same travelling gold arc as the winning Houses, when a faction has won by 13 Houses: only in that state, only with Motion Full and no reduced-motion request (otherwise a still ring, no movement, no layout change). The decision is recorded in section H of the design decisions and supersedes the earlier "never says a faction won" rule.
- **Command Center inspector and cards: results are toasts, every tier square answers, the place and the specialization are one line each, the three tier states have one pair of words, and the count tiles are quiet figures** (fixes #943, #936, #938, #939, #942, #945 and #948; display and wording only: no save write, no request body, no stale guard or backup changed, the recorded requests are unchanged, and the tier removal still takes back nothing). A click on a lower reached tier square used to answer only by writing the same sentence into a result line above the cards (and a copy in the open panel), so the second click changed nothing on the screen and the line was often out of view: now every click on a tier square that cannot write is answered by the app's toast, one at a time, with the board's own words (the board removes tiers with Set tier and Apply tier, one at a time; the Classic list keeps "click the highest reached tier"). The board keeps no result line and no copy of one: a write result is a toast of lines (what changed, what was not written and why, warnings, notes; no closing "Nothing to do." after a write that did something), a notice stays at least six seconds and a long one longer, an error stays until it is dismissed, and a question waiting for an answer is still a question in the page. The tier squares of a card have the Classic list's mouse-over text, built once for both views; the tier states read Not reached, Not collected and Collected everywhere (the Classic list too; the longer explanation stays in the tooltip and the screen-reader text); the place is directly under the House name as the Classic list's own link with its mouse-over and sub-line, and the specialization is one line, "Exploration Specialization", that opens its track; the four counts are quiet read-only figures; the caption paragraph under the board and the chip and note of the Actions block are gone (their words are tooltip and screen-reader text). Tests: `web-tests/lcc-inspector-pass.test.mjs` and the updated board tests; notes in `docs/design/landsraad-command-center/06-m3-fidelity-matrix.md`.
- **Command Center board: the whole action bar is in the control bar above the 5 by 5** (fixes #914; display and focus order only: no save write, no change to what a click sends, asks or says, and the classic list is untouched). Choose all houses and Clear the choice stand in the line of Jump to House (one group that wraps with it); Fill chosen, Complete chosen, Reopen chosen, the faction for Fill and Complete with its note, and the count of the chosen houses (still a live region) are the next line of the bar; the question before a write, the result line and the note on a write stand directly under the bar, before the cards. Nothing is left in a bar under the board, and the collapsed About sections of that bar are not drawn on the board. The controls keep their data attributes, so the same handlers serve them. The keyboard order follows the visual order. Controls are 36 px tall (44 px on a phone or touch screen), the text is 12 px or more, and the bar wraps instead of shrinking; the chips of Show and House are 44 px on a phone. Looked at in a headless browser at 1600, 1280, 1024, 768, 390 and 320 px, dark and light. Not checked in the game.
- **Specialization tab: a track is picked by its name, and the Track numbers section is gone** (fixes the first part of #708, refs #609; slice 1 of 3: the one-XP-field and rest-of-the-tab parts come later; a SAVE WRITE PATH, because the read and the Add XP, Grant max, Reset and Reset all routes now work by the measured number where nothing was assigned; no request body, route or question changed). The editor now knows the number the game uses for each track (1 Crafting, 2 Gathering, 3 Exploration, 4 Combat, 5 Sabotage, measured for #609) where none was assigned, so the tab no longer asks for them: the *Track numbers* card, the *Known a track's number already?* box and its About this are drawn only for a save that has a track whose number cannot be told (for example an earlier assignment that clashes with a known number). The *Advanced: set a raw row* form has a dropdown of the five names instead of a number box and sends the number behind the name; its question names the track. A number assigned earlier still takes the place of the measured one. The read and the Add XP, Grant max and Reset routes use the same numbers, so they work on a save where nothing was ever assigned.
- **Landsraad: the seven older write routes are retired, and one rule says which factions the Landsraad has** (fixes #657, refs #611, #652; a SAVE WRITE PATH, so reviewed before it is merged; the page changes only in its Term panel, last sentence, because it only ever called `/api/landsraad/term`, `/apply` and `/decree`). The routes `/api/landsraad/progress`, `/mine`, `/mine-many`, `/complete`, `/faction-goal`, `/complete-many` and `/reopen-many` wrote the same rows as the board's write but without the rewards, so a raise made through them moved a contribution to a tier with no event and no reward row, and every rule change had to be made twice. They are gone (a request to one now finds no route and writes nothing), with their seven handlers and the tests built around them; the board's one write, `/api/landsraad/apply`, is the only way to change a house. The checks and tests that still matter (the play clock written as a completion time, the repair of older Unix times in the same term, the refusal of a faction the Landsraad does not have, the win a completion takes away) now run through that write. Nothing outside the page called the routes (checked in the page script, the command line, the scripts, the docs and the tests); anything of your own that posted to them now gets a 404 and has to use `/api/landsraad/apply`. **A faction the Landsraad has** is now decided in one place: Atreides and Harkonnen. The game's save also holds the Smuggler faction (id 4), which has no Landsraad rows in any save read; before this, a request that completed a house for it (`complete_for: 4`) was accepted and left a state the game never writes. The list the board offers, the faction of the character, the faction of a completion and the reigning faction of the term all use the same rule now, so Smuggler and None are refused as a bad argument and nothing is written; clearing the reigning faction is still allowed. Also re-checked after #652 (G-019): the page no longer stages changes, and no click asks for a change of your own contribution together with a completion for another faction (a page test now says so), so a request the server would refuse for that reason is not one the page sends. Tests: the faction rule on every write path (Smuggler, None and an unknown id refused, Atreides and Harkonnen accepted), the seven routes answering 404, and the clock and completion tests moved onto the board's write. With the progress route gone, a faction's progress on a house can no longer be set or lowered to an arbitrary amount except through the SQL console; the board's write only raises it, to a completion's goal. A term that already holds a reigning faction the Landsraad does not have is left alone by a request that does not change it, and the Term panel shows such a faction as none and sends no faction when Apply is pressed without choosing one (a request that sets another unoffered faction is still refused).
- **Base export for the console: described as being for the player's own self-hosted server only** (fixes #658, refs #534; page text and docs only, no save write and no change to what the file holds). The export document, the README, the roadmap line, the two button tips and the Bases help text now say the file is for a self-hosted server of your own, not for a console game and not for a public or another person's server, which matches the README's scope (single-player saves on your own PC). The title of #534 was changed the same way.
- **Command Center cards: the specialization badge is a small single-colour emblem, like the House icon** (fixes #963 and closes #884; display only: nothing is written and no request changes; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18; checked in a headless render at 1600, 1024 and 390 px in both themes, not in the game). The diamond badge that used screenshot crops of the approved design is replaced by the path's banner picture (an extracted game interface picture), cut to its centre and drawn in the same one tint as the House crest (gold on the dark look, a darker gold on the light look), in the upper right of the card, with no diamond frame and no progress edge. The name of the path stays as the tooltip and in the card's screen-reader text. The five 84 x 84 crops (art/specs) are no longer used and were deleted, and the art notice and the art manifest no longer list them.
- **Command Center cards: the specialization emblem has no hazy shape behind it any more** (fixes #976; display only: nothing is written and no request changes, the request golden files are unchanged; reported by the operator on 2026-11-18 from a screenshot of build 460; checked in a headless render at 28 px and 24 px in both themes for all five paths, not in the game). The emblem of #963 was the banner picture of the path cut to its centre and zoomed 1.85 times; the banner pictures carry a soft shadow under the emblem and two faded copies of it at the sides, and the House crest's one-tint filter turns every visible pixel to the same gold, so those faint parts showed as a haze behind the emblem and slivers of the side copies at its edges. Five clean emblems are now derived once from the banner art (`internal/web/static/lcc/art/emblems/<path>.png`, 96 x 96 PNG with alpha: only the connected pieces of the centre whose alpha is high, with their soft edge; the shadow and the side copies are dropped; trimmed, padded to a square and scaled down) by `scripts/lcc-emblems.mjs` (run by hand, not in CI; `--check` compares the committed files with its output), and the card draws them at 28 px (24 px on a phone) without the zoom. The pictures are Dune: Awakening game artwork derived from the extracted banner art: the same terms as the banners (`docs/art-notice.md`, `art-manifest.json` role `emblem`). Tests: the lookup (`emblemAsset`), the manifest, the served files, the CSS (no zoom) and the pixels of each emblem (a clear margin, no haze).

- **Command Center cards: the count of reward tiers reads "N/5 EARNED"** (part of #962, the cards only: the list and House Intelligence wording is a follow-up; display text only: nothing is written and no request changes; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18). One pair of words runs through the board: a reward tier of yours is earned, and then collected, which matches the note "Claimed ≠ earned ≠ collected". The card line now reads, for example, "3/5 EARNED ✓ 1 ◆ 2" with the tooltip "Reward tiers of yours earned in this House: 3 of 5"; the screen-reader text of a card, the tooltip of the "no delivery record" count, the explanation for a taken House with nothing of yours and the line under the board say earned instead of reached. The tier state words (NOT REACHED, NOT COLLECTED, COLLECTED) and the list and House Intelligence wording are unchanged in this change.

- **Command Center Specializations: the dossier no longer says the level and the keystone figures again and again** (fixes #1053; display only, no request or save field changed; checked in headless Edge, not in the game). The row of three tiles under the level scale (ASSOCIATED HOUSES, XP CEILING, KEYSTONES) is gone: each repeated a figure beside it (the Houses panel, the end of the XP bar, the keystone bar). The line under the level plate is now just FULL PATH COMPLETE, XP CAP REACHED, KEYSTONES COMPLETE or XP CAP NOT REACHED, without "LEVEL 100 ·" and "KEYSTONES 41/41". Design rows D3 and D8 of the fidelity matrix (`08-m5-specializations.md`) say so.
- **Term and Decrees: a decree row no longer shows its row number, and the pool flag is an Advanced view** (fixes #1054; display only: Set flag sends the same request as before, and the request goldens are unchanged; checked in headless Edge, not in the game). The decree rows of the Command Center section and of the Classic list lose the raw number of the row (`id 4`, the ID column). The pool flag (`POOL FLAG 0/1`) and its Set flag button are hidden until the new button above the pool, "Advanced: show pool flags", is pressed (it then reads "Advanced: hide pool flags"); the choice is kept when the page is drawn again, and pressing it only shows or hides, nothing is written. The Classic list loses the ID, Pool flag and Change columns. Help (Term and Decrees) and the fidelity matrix (`09-m6-decree-term-integration.md`, T5 and T6) say so. The request-equivalence test presses the Advanced button before Set flag.
- **Landsraad: the note "starts on your own faction" beside "Faction for Fill and Complete" is gone** (fixes #1045; wording only: no behaviour, request or save field changed). The option already says "(your faction)". The hover text "The faction of the next Fill faction goal, Complete or bulk click. Choosing it writes nothing." stays, now on the drop-down itself. The same bar serves the Classic list and the Command Center board, so both lose the note.
- **Landsraad and Specializations: the links between them no longer rewrite your saved view, and the Saved map has a way back to the House** (fixes #1060; display and navigation only: nothing is written, no request or save field changed; checked in headless Edge, not in the game). "Open House" on a path's House and "Open in Specializations" in the House Inspector used to save the Command Center view for good (the browser keys `lsb.view` and `spec.view`), so a person on the Classic list was switched for good. They now show the other view for this visit only: the saved choice is untouched, the View switch says which view is shown, and the next time the page is opened the saved view is back. The line "Showing House X on <map>" on the Saved map has a **Back to House X** button that returns to the House on the Landsraad tab in the view the person is in now (the board with the House chosen, or the Classic list with only that House shown and opened). The scroll part of the link is #1041 and is not touched here. New test: `web-tests/crosslinks-view.test.mjs`.
- **Landsraad and Specializations: a figure the save does not hold is a dash (or a plain 0), not the words "Not in the save", "Not recorded" or "not known"** (fixes #1056 for the plaque, Specializations, Term and Decrees and the Classic list; the Inspector and the reward ledger are the other worker's, so see the note below; wording only: no request, route or save field changed; checked in headless Edge, not in the game). The plaque's "HELD BY" and the empty list of a faction's banner show a dash, with "Not recorded in the save" or "Not in the save" as the hover text. On the Specializations view XP, keystones and the totals show a dash (hover: "Not in the save"), and the lane line reads "— XP · — keystones". In Term and Decrees a decree with no effect line shows "Effect: —", and "Ends in" shows a dash when the save gives no play clock. The Classic list shows 0 for a House with no contribution of yours (was "none recorded") and a dash for the XP and level of a track the editor cannot match (was "?"). Still to do under #1056, in the Inspector and the ledger (`lcc-board.js`): "Top contributors: not in the save", "Missions: Not in the save", "your contribution: none recorded", "Specialization not in the save" and the ledger's "not known" tier threshold.
- **Help, Term and Decrees and Specializations: technical wording reworded for players** (refs #1063; wording only: no request, route or save field changed; the Classic Specialization cards are not reworded yet, see below). Help (The Landsraad board) no longer names `board_index` in the opening text and the board header says "The save gives no 5 by 5 board, so the Houses are listed" instead of "Not a fixed board: the Houses are listed as read". Help (Term and Decrees) no longer speaks of "the save's own disabled column"; the pool flag is "a switch the save keeps on each decree". The Specializations hero footnote reads "KEYSTONES (* SOME PATHS SHOW NO TOTAL)" or "(* ONE PATH IS NOT MATCHED YET)", the warnings for a path the editor cannot match say so instead of "no track number of the save is tied to it", the unknown row line reads "The save holds XP in a track the editor does not know (left as it is): track 9", and the Reset all tracks hint and the Help page speak of tracks "the editor can match to a path". The source mark of the Term section no longer lists table names. The names of the save's columns and tables stay in a "Technical notes" part at the end of each Help page. Still to do under #1063: the Classic Specialization list's "Track numbers" card, its hints ("Number not known yet: see Track numbers below") and the "Advanced: set a raw row" card keep their wording, which the uitext tests pin.
- **Term and Decrees, the action bar, the Classic list and the Saved map: standing explanatory lines dropped or moved to hover text** (refs #1062; wording and placement only: no behaviour, request or save field changed; checked in headless Edge, not in the game). Gone: "Nothing is chosen." under the action bar (the live region still speaks the count once something is chosen; the click on a bulk button with nothing chosen still says so), "Click a row to open it." above the Classic list, "any faction can be chosen" under the Reigning faction box (now the hover text of the box, in both views), "not in this term" on every decree row that is not in the term (such a row now shows no badge group), "Scroll the table sideways for House status and Last payout." above the table of an earlier term (the table's region is still named as scrolling sideways), the TERM tile of the Term and Decrees facts (the board header already says "Term 7"), and "The yellow ring on the map marks its place." on the Saved map (now the hover text of the line that names the House; Help says it too). Help (The Landsraad board, Term and Decrees) and the matrix rows T1 and T9 say so. Still to do under #1062 in `lcc-board.js` (the other worker's file): "Favor won ≠ earned ≠ collected", the list caption "The Houses of the term, by default by House name, A to Z", the board footer "Your rewards on this board", and the check and diamond counts under "0/5 EARNED"; "25 of 25 houses shown" is #1044.
### Removed
- **Command Center Specializations: the label "FROM YOUR SAVE · READ WHEN THIS PAGE WAS DRAWN" is gone from the Specialization Lanes header** (fixes #1029; display only, decided by the operator on 2026-11-19: nothing is written and no request or field changed). The header keeps its eyebrow and title; design row H5 in `docs/design/landsraad-command-center/08-m5-specializations.md` is marked as dropped.
- **Command Center: the standing explanations are gone from the page** (#980, #985, #973, #981; text only: every confirm question, refusal and result stays). Out of the House panel: About Fill and the tiers, What the marks mean, Important: three separate states, How each click is written, and the facts text of ACTIONS (tooltip and screen-reader text). Out of the action bar: the line "Writes at once, with a backup first; ... Not checked in the game yet." and the paragraph "Not checked in the game yet. Going back:" of the classic list. Out of Term and Decrees: the intro line (#985), the NOT IN THE SAVE box (#973), the scope line, the pool note, the note inside the closed hidden row, the clock line and the Earlier terms notes. Out of the Specialization view: the lead sentence and three explanation lines (two of them are now tooltips). The key to the board stays (labels only) and is named "Key to the board". All of it is in the Help tab.
- **Command Center Specializations: the "Track mapping and advanced editor" section is removed** (fixes #986; display only: no route, request body or Go code changed, and the write endpoints stay; an operator decision of 2026-11-18; the classic list still has the track numbers, the assign controls and the raw row; checked in the page tests and a headless render, not in the game). The eyebrow, heading, the Advanced controls disclosure, the list of track numbers, assigning an unidentified number, entering a number by hand and the exact raw row of XP and level are gone from the Command Center view, and so are the two pointers to it (the XP editing notes of a path without a number now say plainly that no track number of the save is tied to it). Nothing replaces the section: XP is changed in the Specialization Operations panel as before. When the save holds a row that no path owns, one read-only line says "Unknown specialization row in the save (not edited)" with its number. The recorded-request test still plays the track mapping and raw row scenarios against the classic list and the file is unchanged.
- **Command Center: the row of four figures under the plaque is removed** (fixes #935; display only: nothing is written, no request, field or Go code changed; an operator-requested change of 2026-11-18; not checked in the game). TERM STATUS, OPEN HOUSES, POTENTIAL LINES and SPECIALIZATION CAP are gone: the state of the term is on the plaque, the open Houses are in its tally line, and the specialization cap stays on the Specializations tab. The Term and Decrees section and the board panel come up under the plaque. The one figure that lost its place, how many of the twelve lines each faction can still make, is now one line of the Imperial Victory Analysis ("Lines still open: 4 · Atreides 4 · Harkonnen 0", or "count incomplete" with no number when a House cannot be read).
- **Command Center: Sysselraad Strategy and the Clear highlight button are removed** (#958; display only: nothing is written, no request, field or Go code changed, and the request goldens are unchanged; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18). The Sysselraad Strategy button, the "Twelve lines" list with its order switch and Jump to missing House, the violet preview outline and its card words, the PREVIEW LINE legend entry and the Escape handling for a chosen line are gone, with their styles, tests and rows in the design documents. The Clear highlight button went too: it only let go of the House being looked at and the win outlines stayed; choosing another House, choosing the same House again and Escape still move or drop the selection. The twelve-line check stays, because the gold win outline, the plaque and the victory panel are worked out from it.
- **Command Center: no SYSSELRAAD WON flag on the winning House cards** (fixes #961 and closes #946; display only: nothing is written and no request changes; requested by the operator on 2026-11-18). A faction with a line of five or 13 Houses had the same flag on every winning card (thirteen of them on a 13-House win), and it wrapped at narrow widths and made cards taller. The one gold outline stays and carries the win. The words are said once, in the plaque, the victory panel, the legend key (WINNING HOUSES), and in the tooltip and the screen-reader text of each winning card.

### Fixed
- **Player > Vehicles and Bases: a Deep Desert row names its place, "Deep Desert, G5", where the Hagga Basin says "near <region>"** (fixes #1175; display and wording only: the read gained one field, `place`, and nothing is written; not checked in the game). The Deep Desert has no region names, so a vehicle or base there showed its coordinates and no place, and the list did not say which map it was on. It now says "Deep Desert" and the square of the game's lettered grid the position is in, from the existing `kit/ddgrid` (the letter is the row counted from the north, A to I, the number the column counted from the west, 1 to 9; the same squares the Landsraad cards already name). A position outside the grid says "Deep Desert" alone. Hagga Basin rows keep "near <region>", and a stored vehicle keeps its stored tag. The Bases list shares the code and has the same wording. The Live map's region table is unchanged.
- **Player > Bases: Back up and remove refuses a base in the Deep Desert** (refs #1177; SAVE WRITE PATH, so reviewed before it is merged; the edit only gets stricter, nothing new is written). The game does not take a base backup of a base there, so a backup row for one would be a state the game never produces. The preview and the write both refuse a base whose totem is on a Deep Desert map, with the plain message, and change nothing; the button on those rows is greyed out and its tooltip says why. A base in Hagga Basin works as before. A recycle for Deep Desert bases is planned (#1176).
- **The banner that says edits are queued is short: what is waited for and when the edits are written** (refs #1128; display and wording only: the waits, the gate and the timings are unchanged, nothing else is written or read differently; not checked in the game). It read "Changes you make now are queued. The game wrote its database 21 seconds ago, the last write after the single-player session ended 24 seconds ago; the logs show it then stays silent, so the editor waits 30 seconds after the end and 60 seconds after that write (about 39 seconds more); edits stay queued until this clears, or the game closes. They are written by themselves as soon as that clears." It now reads "Edits are queued. The game saved 21 seconds ago; they are written in about 39 seconds, or when the game closes." Every wait of the same gate follows the same pattern ("you left the world 4 minutes ago", "the save file changed 20 seconds ago", "a single-player session is active; they are written when this clears, or when the game closes"). The technical account (the last write, the end time, the grace values) is kept word for word and moved under the banner's Details line (the state answer carries it as `blockDetail`). The automatic refill's waiting line says the same short fact.
- **After leaving a world the editor waits about a minute again, not three, when the game made two writes just after leaving** (refs #1122; SAVE WRITE GATE, so reviewed before it is merged; nothing is written differently once the gate lets a write through; not checked in the game). The log of 2026-11-22 00:14 UTC shows the exit that cost three minutes: the periodic write of the game fell 0.4 s after the end marker and the write it makes when it leaves came 3.6 s after it, and the gate allowed exactly one write after the marker. Re-measured in all the logs kept on this computer: ten exits, nine with one write 1.8 s to 3.8 s after the marker and this one with two. Now one write, or two that both fall within 10 s of the marker, is the normal exit and the 30 s and 60 s waits apply; a second write later than that, three or more writes, no end marker, and no write after the marker keep the 3-minute and 6-minute waits, and the 3-minute quiet time itself is not shortened. The table is in docs/design/2026-10-27-menu-write-grace.md.
- **Base file export refuses a file made from two states of the save** (refs #613, audit D3-2; read only: nothing in the save is written). The export reads the save's tables one after the other and used to notice a change between the reads only by the number of queued edits, so the file read again, or a queued edit dropped, between two reads left the number unchanged and gave a file that mixed two states. The save now keeps a revision number that changes with every edit that changed rows and every time the file is read again, and the export (the base file and the two console files) refuses, with the same words as before, when it differs at the end. The value checks of the same issue (stacks, slots, health, durability) were already in #629 and #769.
- **Landsraad Command Center: the House Intelligence panel is held on the same line as the board's control strip while the page scrolls** (fixes #1143; layout only: nothing is written, no request, save field or count changed and the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; measured in headless Edge on a static copy of the page with the synthetic answer at 1280 x 800 and 1920 x 1080, not in the game). Held under the header, the panel stood 12 px lower than the control strip of the board; its top edge is now the strip's top edge (one offset, `--hdr-h` plus the room of the warning bars) and it still keeps 12 px to the bottom of the window. The two columns, the order of the side column (victory analysis, then the panel) and the inner scroll of the panel are as before.
- **Landsraad Command Center: the words Atreides and Harkonnen under the two faction icons of the action bar are gone** (fixes #1142; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or count changed and the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; measured in headless Edge on a static copy of the page, not in the game). The icons are known, the words crowded the bar. Each icon keeps its accessible name, its mouse-over text, its pressed state and its 44 px size; the room that was kept under the icons for the words is removed, so the icons now sit in line with Fill, Complete and Reopen (about 7 px lower than before) and the bar keeps its height. Under 700 px the words were already hidden and nothing changes.
- **Landsraad: the specialization emblem is shown in House Intelligence and in the List's Specialization column** (fixes #1145; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or count changed and the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; checked in jsdom, not in the game). The House panel draws the path's emblem, the same picture the 5x5 card draws (28 px, from one helper now shared by the card, the panel and the list, no new artwork), to the left of the specialization line; the line, the Level text and the link to the Specializations tab are as before. The List's Specialization column has a 20 px emblem to the left of the name; the words stay. A House with no track keeps the dash and gets no icon.
- **Landsraad: Find House also opens the House in House Intelligence, in the List and in the Grid** (fixes #1147; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or count changed and the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; checked in jsdom, not in the game). The pick goes to the House, outlines it for 1.8 s as before and chooses it, as a click on its square (Grid) or its name (List) does, so the panel opens on it. It still changes no filter and ticks no box (the Choose column stays the multi-select for actions). Finding the House that is already chosen keeps it chosen. A House the List hides under the current filter has no row, and then nothing happens, as before. The mouse-over text of the key now reads "Find House: goes to one House and opens it in House Intelligence".
- **Landsraad List view: the House found by Find House is marked as one row, not cell by cell** (fixes #1146; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or count changed and the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; checked in jsdom and in a headless browser on a plain table, not in the game). The 3 px line in the focus colour now goes around the whole row, so there are no lines between its cells; it is an outline, so a forced-colours page keeps a visible mark. The colour, the 1.8 s and the trigger are as before, and the mark of a House card in the 5x5 grid is not changed.
- **Landsraad Command Center: the House Intelligence panel shows the place once, the LOCATION section at the bottom is gone** (fixes #1144; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or count changed and the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; checked in jsdom, not in the game). The heading, the place line, the "From you" line, the note for an unconfirmed place and the "Open in the Saved map" button at the foot of the panel are removed, and nothing replaces them. The place button under the House name at the top is unchanged and still opens the Saved map or says why it cannot; the distance from you is still in the mouse-over of the place in the list and on the card. The two-column layout of the panel (860 to 1079 px of tab) loses the place from its right column, which now holds the two scales.
- **The editor keeps its watch of the save file by itself, so hiding the window no longer makes a by-hand pass wait 60 seconds again** (fixes #1139; a SAVE WRITE GATE path, so reviewed before it is merged; no gate is weakened, no rule or wait changed; checked by Go tests with the game, the log, the file time and the clock faked, not in the game). With the game open, the editor writes only after it has watched the save file unchanged for 60 seconds, looking at least every 30. With nothing queued, the only thing that looked was the page, and the page stops while its window is hidden or minimised, so a Tau window behind the game for more than 30 seconds broke the watch and the first look after you came back began a new 60-second wait, however old the file was. The editor now also looks by itself, every 10 seconds while a game process exists, from the loop that already runs every 3 seconds. It writes nothing and takes none of the save's locks; a real gap (sleep, hibernation, a clock change), a change of the file's time, an active single-player session and every other rule of the gate still hold the write back, and the gates that decide a write are asked as before.
- **Give item keeps everything that was entered after an error, and after any redraw of the page** (fixes #1148; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or setting changed; checked by page tests, not in the game). The Augments block of Give item (the grade and the slots) was drawn only when the item picker changed, never by the card itself, so when the page was drawn again (its own refresh while the game saves, or any action that redraws) it came back with the item still in the picker and an empty block, and the augment grade and slots were lost: after the error "the same augment twice" the person had to choose the item again, or leave the tab and come back, and set everything again. The block is now drawn again for the item in the picker and its grade and slots get back what they held, with the quantity and the item's grade, which were already kept. The care package editor's item row, which shares the block, is the same. A refused Give, or an Add to queue with no item, changes nothing that was entered.
- **A queued Give is listed by what it gives, not as "Give 1 queued items"** (fixes #1149; wording of the waiting-changes list only: nothing else is written, no request, save field or setting changed; checked by Go and page tests, not in the game). The list "What is queued" in the banner said only how many items a queued Give held. Each queued Give now names the item, its quantity, its grade and its augments with the grade they are added at, for example "Give 1 x Ambition (grade 5, augments: A, B at grade 3)"; a queue of several items is one entry with one line to an item, and its tooltip says it all on one line. A give that only partly fitted says which line fell short ("340 of 500 x ..."), as a single Give does. The same wording is what the write log records for a Give queue.
- **"Run the automatic pass now" is queued, not refused, while the game is open** (fixes #1135; a SAVE WRITE GATE path, so reviewed before it is merged; no gate is weakened; checked by Go tests with the game states faked, not in the game). When the check for an edit made by hand blocked the pass (the game open with the save just seen, a session active, the game wrote a moment ago, the file open elsewhere), the button answered in red "The automatic pass was not run: ... Run it again once that clears." Now it is queued the same way the pass at launch is and says one line, "Queued: runs by itself when the save is free." (shown with the neutral queued mark, not the check); the reason shows in the queue view and on the Automatic refill card (also with the master switch off), and Cancel in the queue view stops it. A queued by-hand pass runs under the rules of the pass at launch, because nobody is looking when it runs later: it waits for every game process to be gone (an open game at its menu does not let it through), it is written as "automatic pass" in the write log (not "run by hand"), and it runs on the file as the game left it. It ignores the master switch, as a by-hand pass does, and stops if every job is switched off while it waits. A by-hand pass that the check lets through is unchanged: it runs at once with the check of an edit made by hand. The hint of the Run button and the About text of the card no longer say that the pass waits "with the game still open". The test that asserted the old refusal now asserts the queue; README and the two design notes say the same. Proved by `internal/web/autopass_byhand_queue_test.go`.
- **Landsraad Command Center: the mouse-over text of Find House (and of Filter) no longer stays on screen after a House is chosen** (fixes #1134; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or count changed and the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; checked in jsdom, not in the game). When the Find House list closes, the focus returns to the key, and the style sheet shows a key's text for as long as it has the focus. The key that gets the focus back is now quiet: it shows no text until the mouse comes over it again or a key is pressed. The Filter key, which closes the same way (Done, Escape), gets the same fix. Proved by `web-tests/lcc-tip-after-close-1134.test.mjs`.
- **Give item and the item's Augments card: the grade sits with the Augments heading and the slots are one under the other, the same width** (fixes #1136; layout only: nothing is written, no request, save field or choice changed, and there is still ONE grade for all the added augments; not checked in the game). The row read "Augments Slot 1 [list]" on one line and "Slot 2 [list]" with the grade on the next, so the two lists started in different places and the grade looked like part of slot 2. Now an "Augments" heading row holds the one grade, and the slots are under it, one per line, labelled Slot 1 and Slot 2, with lists of one width. The care package editor draws the same block. In the item's Augments card the grade moved up to that heading row, so its hint says "the grade above" instead of "the grade below". Proved by `web-tests/give-augments-layout-1136.test.mjs`.
- **Give item: the picker is wide enough for a whole name, and an item and its schematic are two different rows** (refs #1126; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or catalog entry changed; not checked in the game). The Give item box is wider (never wider than its row, so a phone is unchanged), and a schematic row reads "Item name (schematic)" next to the plain "Item name" row. The queued line says "(item)" for an item that also has a schematic and "(schematic)" for a schematic, and the result says "Schematic added" or "Item added" (a queue says how many of its lines were schematics). A name only a schematic has is still found by that name.
- **The queue view lists a waiting automatic refill, the Discard queue button is only drawn when there is something to discard, and the queued-refill note is one short sentence** (refs #1123; the page and two small routes: a read-only field in the status the page polls and a Cancel that stops a refill from waiting; nothing is written to the save and no setting changes; not checked in the editor). The button had the module's display set on it, which beat its hidden attribute, so Discard queue showed with nothing queued; it is hidden again with nothing to discard. An automatic refill that waits for the game is now a line of "What is queued" (what it is, what it waits for, no countdown) with a Cancel refill button, and the chip counts it; it is not one of the queued edits, so the banner sentences and Discard queue still speak of edits only. The note "Automatic refill queued: ..." is the toast "Refill queued; it runs when the save is free." with a neutral queued mark instead of the green check; the reason is said in the banner's list.
- **Landsraad Command Center: the mouse-over of Filter and Find House no longer covers the count line or reaches over House Intelligence; the House list has an All choice; Location, not Place; the count line no longer reads "25 / 25"** (refs #1113, #1114, #1115, #1116; display only: nothing is written, no request, save field or count changed and the recorded-request golden files are unchanged; checked in jsdom and in headless Edge at 1280 px with the synthetic answers, not in the game). The mouse-over text of a key in the control strip (Show, Filter, Find House, the count, Choose shown) now opens under the whole strip, as wide as its text up to the width of the strip, so it covers neither the count line, the chips nor the other keys, and never runs on over the House Intelligence panel. In the Filter menu the House list starts with All (it ticks every House that is shown, or clears the choice when every shown House is ticked) and the House row that said Any says All. The House Intelligence section and the list column are called Location, as the Help says. With no filter narrowing the board the count line says "All 25 shown" next to Choose shown (it was "25 / 25", which read as if all 25 were chosen); a filter that narrows the board says "9 / 25 shown".
- **The tag of a House with no tier record keeps to one line in the Inspector ledger** (refs #1097, #1090, #1093; display only: nothing is written, no request or save field changed, and no request golden file changed; checked in headless Edge at 1280 px wide, not in the game). The tag "PLEDGED · NO TIER RECORD" wrapped to two lines beside the ledger heading; it now stays on one line and drops under the heading when it does not fit beside it. The rest of what the three issues ask was already on main (#1052, #1055 and #1059, merged in #1065): one House count in the Victory panel, "Unlisted item" instead of an item id, and short list cells with an em dash for a House with no place.
- **The hover peek of a House's place draws the real map, not a generic octagon, and the House card starts with the large House symbol** (fixes #1069; display only: nothing is written, no request or save field changed, and no request golden file changed; checked in headless Edge at 1280 and 390 px, dark and light, not in the game). The quick peek (the hover of the place in the Classic list and in the Command Center list, the Place line of the House card, the Inspector's place and the Where part of an opened Classic row) now shows a zoomed window of the real picture of the House's map (Hagga Basin, the Deep Desert, Arrakeen or Harko Village) around the Saved map's own ring, with the whole map small in a corner away from the pin and the window marked on it, above the unchanged Region and From you lines. The pin is the same fraction of the picture as on the Saved map, and a test works it back out of the drawn page against the map space in `internal/ops/livemap.go`. The pictures are the program's own files, lazy (nothing is asked for until the peek is shown), with no network request and no inlined picture. The octagon remains only for a place outside the frame of its picture and for a map the editor has no picture of, with a 12 px caption that says which; the note under it no longer says there is no picture when the editor has it. The peek also opens on keyboard focus of the place link and Escape puts it away; the narrow window keeps its bottom sheet and the click through to the place (#1025). The House hover card (Classic, and the Command Center list and grid) starts with the large House symbol from the Command Center art (128 px, shown at 64), the name and the region, with the existing lines unchanged. The Quick peek line is 12 px. There is no swatch hover: the editor owns no picture of the House swatches (`docs/art-notice.md`). Help and the fidelity matrix (rows P1 to P5) are updated.
- **"Back to House X" on the Saved map, and "Open House" of a path on the Specializations page, open the Landsraad tab at the House, not at the top of the page** (fixes #1072; display and navigation only: nothing is written, no request or save field changed, the request golden files are unchanged; checked in headless Edge at 1280 and 390 px, not in the game). The links chose the House (the Inspector showed it) but nothing scrolled the page to it: a move to another page starts at the top (#1025), and the page had no scroll to a House of its own after it was drawn, so a long board stayed at its top with the House far below. The fix is in the shared place: a link that opens a page at one of its parts now asks for the scroll before it moves (`askReveal`), and `render()` runs that scroll once, after the page is drawn and the view is put back, so it is the last scroll and nothing undoes it. The Landsraad links bring the House's card (the board's grid or list) or its row (the Classic list) into view below the sticky header and put the keyboard focus on it, as Jump to House does. The Specialization links (#1041) now use the same mechanism and still open at the lanes; Jump to House, the place links to the Saved map and the saved view choice (#1060) are unchanged. New test: `web-tests/back-to-house-scroll.test.mjs`; the headless sweep (`scripts/lcc-m7-sweep.mjs`, case `maplink`) now also presses Back to House on a scrolled board, list and Classic list and checks the House is in view.
- **A place link of a House opens the Saved map at the map, not at the bottom of the page** (fixes #1025; display and navigation only: nothing is written, no request or save field changed; checked in headless Edge, not in the game). Every link that opens the Saved map from the Landsraad (the Classic row and its hover card, the Command Center list link, hover cards and inspector, Jump to House) landed on the right map and the right place, but the page kept the scroll offset of the board it had left, and the Saved map is a shorter page: it ended up at its bottom, at the table of marker types, with no map in view. A move to another page now starts at the top (as the code always said it did), and the Saved map opened from a House scrolls its map card into view, clear of the sticky header, with the keyboard focus on the map. A line above the map says which House it shows (with a Stop showing it button), and the pin has the House name beside its ring. A click on the small peek map under a place (a sheet over the link in a narrow window) now opens the Saved map as the place link does.
- **Review follow-ups of the Landsraad work: held rename, chosen Houses after a re-read, Unfill, the paid-ground record and the Specializations notes** (fixes #1019, #1013, #1011, #1010, #1006 in part; a SAVE WRITE PATH, so reviewed before it is merged; nothing checked in the game). (1) The check before a retry of a held save rename reads the file up to three times before it gives up, and when it still cannot, the message keeps the rename's own error and the hint that another program may be holding the file (#1019). (2) After a write and the re-read the chosen Houses are pruned against what the board shows, so "N chosen" and the phone bar never count a dimmed House; the Term Apply and Make active handler asks the busy check before it answers for a button that is off (#1019). (3) Unfill: the help text names the limit of a waiting reward the game wrote under the same item, the summary counts only Houses that have an unfill plan, and the recorded result lines of Unfill in the request golden come from a lowering answer, not a raise-shaped one (only those two `said` lines changed; no request did) (#1013). (4) Paid-ground record: a take-back note only raises the ceiling of its faction's entry and no longer replaces that entry's own lower stretch by the other faction's (the note carries a new `backOnly` flag; an older build that does not know it merges the note as before, which only loses ground); the prune of the copies moved aside keeps exactly the newest ten and the copy just made, also when the clock steps back; the folder is flushed through the hook of the options (#1011, #1010). Tests: the new cases fail on the old code. The size limit of the record file went from 1 MiB to 3.25 MiB (#840): an older build that reads a record over 1 MiB moves it aside and starts a new one (practically unreachable: real records are about 600 KB or less) (#1010). Not done, on the issues: #1013 items 2 and 3, #1010 items 4, 5, 6 and 9, #1006 items 1 and 4, #1019 items 2 and 7; #770 was already done by #786.
- **A flaky watch test, the CI slowest-package report with a faster internal/ops run, the Create a vehicle repeat-check tests and two page races of its part picker** (fixes #875, #767, #793, #742, #739; tests, CI, comments and page code only: no save write, no request body and no route that writes changed; not checked in the game). The test of the watch kept while a write is prepared now sends the ticks of `keepWatching` itself, one at a time, so a slow runner cannot reorder an evaluation behind the write's own (the one new seam is the variable `watchTicks` in `internal/save/filetime.go`, a `time.Ticker` as shipped). CI prints the slowest packages of the race run and warns past half the 20-minute limit; the slowest 25 tests of `internal/ops` run side by side; the job limit is 40 minutes. The game's vehicle in the repeat-check tests now carries its entity links, with a case of another class under the Create's own links; the assumption that the game keeps a vehicle's class and links across a save is written down in the design note with an in-game check (not done). A part pick that is refused while the page is drawn again no longer shows the refused part, and a pick answered after the page was left no longer redraws the other page or toasts there.
- **Landsraad: a dimmed House can no longer be chosen or written in bulk** (fixes #881; SAVE WRITE PATH, so reviewed before it is merged; not checked in the game). On the board the choose box of a card the filter dims is off and says so, a box turned on anyway is taken back, and a bulk button (and the Yes of its question) writes only Houses the page shows now: a House still in the choice list that the board or the Classic list does not show is dropped first, and a choice left with nothing says so and writes nothing. The request itself is unchanged (the server cannot know the page's filter; it already refuses a House twice in one request and any House that is not what the page showed).
- **Term and decrees: leftovers of the M6 review** (fixes #904; no request or save field changed). A click on Make active or Apply while a write is running now changes nothing, so the Active decree drop-down keeps showing the decree of the write that runs; the tags row of the term section is a group with a name. The other points of the issue (the demo badge, the Sysselraad Strategy Escape) were already gone with the sample decree and the strategy panel.
- **A held rename of the save is not retried over a file the game wrote meanwhile** (fixes #794; SAVE WRITE PATH, so reviewed before it is merged; nothing checked against the real game). Before each retry of a held rename the editor reads the save again and compares its hash; a changed or unreadable file ends the tries with nothing replaced, the temp file removed and the edits still queued. kit/durable: new `Options.BeforeRetry`.
- **Command Center look and feel: contributor names 12 px, the win glow stays in the gap, the 11 px flag, and the leftovers of the polish review** (fixes #918, #907, #895, #910; display only: nothing is written and no request, field, route or Go code changed; not checked in the game). Contributor names on the wide plaque are never under 12 px (the text size no longer shrinks to 11 px between about 940 and 1130 px of plaque width): a whole entry that does not fit is out of sight, as #972 set, so a narrow banner shows fewer names and the full ranking stays in the mouse-over text of its heading; the TOP CONTRIBUTORS heading stays 11 px. The glow of a winning House card reaches no further than the gap between the cards (10, 8 or 4 px, by layout) and its pulse follows, so it no longer tints the edge of a neighbour; the line badge that #895 also names is not drawn on a card any more (#961), so there was nothing to reserve. `.lcc-flag` is 11 px and the known-size list of the M7 test is empty. From #910: the room that the warning bars under the header take is measured (`--warn-h`), not a constant 76 px, so a tall bar of queued writes no longer hides a jump or a focus; the selector list that a browser without `:has()` dropped whole is split; the table of an earlier term is a tab stop only while it scrolls sideways; the Specializations inputs and buttons are 44 px at a phone width, not only on a touch screen; with three lanes to a row the last two sit in the middle; a code comment no longer says "win token".
